Specifically, Visual Correlation Enhancement (VCE) and Text Semantic Augmentation (TSA) modules are proposed for dense prediction enhancement. To improve the spatial awareness of visual features, our VCE module utilizes diffusion's reliable spatial consistency to mitigate the over-smoothing issue in CLIP's attention. It designs the Attention Clustering Refinement (ACR) module to reliably extract diverse correlation maps from the diffusion model. The correlation maps act as a diversity bias for CLIP's self-attention, recursively pushing its visual features towards a more discriminative dense distribution. To augment the semantics of text embeddings, our TSA module argues that a single text modality is insufficient to encompass the variability of visual categories. Thus, we leverage diffusion's generative power to maintain a dynamic key-value cache model, shifting CAM generation from a patch-text matching mechanism to a novel visual knowledge retrieval paradigm. With these enhancements, DiCLIP not only outperforms state-of-the-art methods on PASCAL VOC and MS COCO but also significantly reduces training costs. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/zwyang6/DiCLIP.

</details>


### [38] [Reference-based Category Discovery: Unsupervised Object Detection with Category Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04606)
*Yichen Li,Qiankun Liu,Ying Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RefCD是一种无需人工标注的参考基于类别发现的无监督检测器，通过预测对象与无标签参考图像之间的特征相似性实现类别感知检测，同时支持无参考图像的类别无关检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统一次性检测方法解决了闭集检测问题，但数据标注成本高昂。现有的通用无监督方法生成的伪边界框没有类别标签，无法实现类别感知分类。需要一种无需标注就能实现类别感知检测的方法。

Method: 提出参考基于类别发现（RefCD）无监督检测器，通过预测对象与无标签参考图像之间的特征相似性实现类别感知检测。设计了特征相似性损失函数来明确指导潜在类别特定特征的学习。同时支持无需参考图像的类别无关检测，形成一个统一框架。

Result: 通过全面的定量和定性分析表明，RefCD在类别感知和类别无关检测方面都表现出有效性。该方法可以在无监督范式中学习类别信息，即使没有类别标签。

Conclusion: RefCD成功解决了无监督检测中类别感知的挑战，通过参考图像的特征相似性实现了无需标注的类别发现和检测，为无监督目标检测提供了新的有效方法。

Abstract: Traditional one-shot detection methods have addressed the closed-set problem in object detection, but the high cost of data annotation remains a critical challenge. General unsupervised methods generate pseudo boxes without category labels, thus failing to achieve category-aware classification. To overcome these limitations, we propose Reference-based Category Discovery (RefCD), an unsupervised detector that enables category-aware\footnotemark[1] detection without any manually annotated labels. It leverages feature similarity between predicted objects and unlabeled reference images. Unlike previous unsupervised methods that lack category guidance and one-shot methods which require labeled data, RefCD introduces a carefully designed feature similarity loss to explicitly guide the learning of potential category-specific features. Additionally, RefCD supports category-agnostic detection without reference images, serving as a unified framework. Comprehensive quantitative and qualitative analysis of category-aware and category-agnostic detection results demonstrates its effectiveness, and RefCD can learn category information in an unsupervised paradigm even without category labels.

</details>


### [39] [Advancing Aesthetic Image Generation via Composition Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04609)
*Kai Zou,Zhiwei Zhao,Bin Liu,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Composer是一个基于美学理论的框架，以语义无关的方式显式建模图像构图，支持构图转移和主题驱动的构图检索，显著提升文本到图像生成的美学质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构图是视觉美学的核心要素，但现有方法通常通过隐式学习或基于语义的布局控制来增强构图，而不是显式地建模构图本身。构图原则虽然独立于具体内容，但在实践中常与语义耦合，这限制了构图控制的精度和灵活性。

Method: Composer框架采用语义无关的构图建模：1) 构图转移：从参考图像提取构图感知表示，通过定制的条件引导模块控制预训练扩散模型的构图；2) 主题驱动构图检索：利用大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）的上下文学习能力进行显式构图规划；3) 无参考构图增强：在训练好的控制模块上进行文本到构图微调实现隐式构图规划；4) 使用最先进生成模型构建了200万图像-文本对的高质量数据集。

Result: 实验结果表明，Composer在文本到图像任务中显著提升了美学质量，支持个性化的构图控制和转移，为用户提供了创作过程中的精确性和灵活性。

Conclusion: Composer成功填补了构图显式建模的研究空白，通过语义无关的方式实现了有效的构图控制和转移，为图像生成任务提供了更高水平的美学控制和创作灵活性，有望推动视觉内容创作的发展。

Abstract: Composition is a cornerstone of visual aesthetics, influencing the appeal of an image. While its principles operate independently of specific content, in practice, composition is often coupled with semantics. As a result, existing methods often enhance composition either through implicit learning or by semantics-based layout control, rather than explicitly modeling composition itself. To address this gap, we introduce Composer, a framework rooted in aesthetic theory, designed to model composition in a semantic-agnostic manner. First, it supports composition transfer by extracting key composition-aware representations from a reference image and leveraging a tailored conditional guidance module to control composition based on pre-trained diffusion models. Second, when users specify only text themes without a composition reference, Composer supports theme-driven composition retrieval by leveraging the in-context learning capabilities of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs), achieving explicit composition planning. To enhance composition in a reference-free mode, we conduct text-to-composition fine-tuning on the trained control module to enable implicit composition planning. Furthermore, we curated a high-quality dataset comprising 2 million image-text pairs using state-of-the-art generative models to support model training. Experimental results demonstrate that Composer significantly enhances aesthetic quality in text-to-image tasks and facilitates personalized composition control and transfer, offering users precision and flexibility in the creative process.

</details>


### [40] [Temporal Structure Matters for Efficient Test-Time Adaptation in Wearable Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04617)
*Zishu Zhou,Zaipeng Xie,Xuanyao Jie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SIGHT是一个轻量级、无需反向传播的测试时自适应框架，通过利用可穿戴活动识别数据的时间结构来实现实时边缘部署，显著提升跨用户分布偏移下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴人类活动识别模型在现实跨用户分布偏移下性能下降，现有测试时自适应方法主要继承视觉任务假设，未能充分利用WHAR数据流固有的时间窗口间结构特征。

Method: 提出SIGHT框架：将时间结构重新定义为特征条件推理信号而非仅输出空间平滑先验；通过对比当前特征与基于原型的期望状态计算预测惊奇度；利用特征偏差指导基于原型对齐和流级边缘习惯跟踪的几何感知过渡路由。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的评估表明，SIGHT优于现有TTA基线方法，同时显著降低计算和内存成本。

Conclusion: 时间连续性和观测诱导的特征偏差为确定何时保持或释放时间惯性以及在哪里路由预测细化提供了互补线索，这使得SIGHT成为适用于WHAR的有效轻量级测试时自适应框架。

Abstract: Wearable human activity recognition (WHAR) models often suffer from performance degradation under real-world cross-user distribution shifts. Test-time adaptation (TTA) mitigates this degradation by adapting models online using unlabeled test streams, yet existing methods largely inherit assumptions from vision tasks and underexploit the inherent inter-window temporal structure in WHAR streams. In this paper, we revisit such temporal structure as a feature-conditioned inference signal rather than merely an output-space smoothing prior. We derive the insight that temporal continuity and observation-induced feature deviations provide complementary cues for determining when to preserve or release temporal inertia and where to route prediction refinement during likely transitions. Building upon this insight, we propose SIGHT, a lightweight and backpropagation-free TTA framework for WHAR, enabling real-time edge deployment. SIGHT estimates predictive surprise by comparing the current feature with a prototype-based expected state, and then uses the resulting feature deviation to guide geometry-aware transition routing based on prototype alignment and stream-level marginal habit tracking. Evaluations on real-world datasets confirm that SIGHT outperforms existing TTA baselines while reducing computational and memory costs.

</details>


### [41] [UniPCB: A Generation-Assisted Detection Framework for PCB Defect Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04635)
*Huan Zhang,Lianghong Tan,Yichu Xu,Jiangzhong Cao,Huanqi Wu,Linwei Zhu,Xu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UniPCB框架，通过多模态条件生成器合成PCB缺陷样本，结合倒置残差移位注意力和跨层互补融合模块进行缺陷检测，解决样本稀缺和特征表示不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCB缺陷检测面临两个主要挑战：1) 缺陷样本稀缺且不平衡，限制了模型训练；2) 在复杂电路背景下特征表示不足。现有生成方法依赖单模态条件控制粗糙，检测方法只改进架构而不解决数据瓶颈。

Method: 1) 生成端：多模态条件生成器提取边缘、深度和文本条件，ScaleEncoder将这些条件嵌入扩散U-Net，Condition Modulation使用FiLM风格空间自适应调制实现结构对齐的缺陷感知合成；2) 检测端：倒置残差移位注意力耦合自注意力和移位卷积捕捉全局上下文和局部纹理，跨层互补融合块生成像素级门控选择跨层特征融合。

Result: 在DsPCBSD+数据集上，UniPCB缺陷检测达到mAP@0.5 98.0%和mAP@0.5:0.95 61.8%，超越所有对比方法。生成分支FID为129.61，SSIM为0.619，优于现有条件生成方法。

Conclusion: UniPCB框架通过生成辅助的PCB缺陷检测，同时解决了样本稀缺和特征表示问题。生成合成的样本直接丰富检测训练集，使生成和检测改进相互促进，为PCB缺陷检测提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Printed Circuit Board (PCB) defect inspection faces two compounding challenges: scarce and imbalanced defect samples that limit model training, and insufficient feature representation under complex circuit backgrounds. Existing generation methods rely on single-modality conditions with coarse structural control, while detection methods improve architectures without addressing the data bottleneck. To resolve both challenges jointly, we propose a generation-assisted PCB defect inspection framework that integrates controlled defect synthesis with task-specific defect detection. On the generation side, a Multi-modal Condition Generator extracts complementary edge, depth, and text conditions in parallel. A ScaleEncoder then embeds these conditions into the diffusion U-Net at four resolutions, and a Condition Modulation applies FiLM-style spatially-adaptive modulation at each scale, enabling structurally aligned and defect-aware sample synthesis. On the detection side, an Inverted Residual Shift Attention couples self-attention with shift-wise convolution to jointly capture global context and local texture, and a Cross-level Complementary Fusion Block generates pixel-level gates for selective cross-level feature fusion. The synthesized samples directly enrich the detection training set, so that improvements in generation compound with improvements in detection. Extensive experiments on DsPCBSD+ demonstrate that UniPCB achieves mAP@0.5 of 98.0% and mAP@0.5:0.95 of 61.8% on defect detection, surpassing all compared methods, while the generation branch attains an FID of 129.61 and SSIM of 0.619, outperforming existing conditional generation approaches.

</details>


### [42] [CAST: Mitigating Object Hallucination in Large Vision-Language Models via Caption-Guided Visual Attention Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04641)
*Qiming Li,Zekai Ye,Xiaocheng Feng,Weihong Zhong,Libo Qin,Ruihan Chen,Lei Huang,Baohang Li,Kui Jiang,Yaowei Wang,Ting Liu,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAST是一种无需训练、即插即用的幻觉缓解方法，通过利用标题查询的注意力激活模式来增强大型视觉语言模型的视觉感知能力，有效减少物体幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在下游任务中表现出色，但经常产生偏离视觉信息的内容，导致物体幻觉。现有方法依赖昂贵的标注和训练成本，或使用解码策略显著增加推理时间。作者观察到LVLMs在回答标题查询时对视觉信息的注意力明显增强。

Method: 提出Caption-guided Visual Attention Steering (CAST)方法：1）使用探测技术识别对标题查询高度敏感的注意力头；2）估计其输出的优化引导方向；3）通过这种引导增强LVLMs的细粒度视觉感知能力，缓解物体幻觉。该方法无需训练，即插即用。

Result: CAST在五个广泛使用的LVLMs和五个基准测试（包括判别性和生成性任务）中，平均减少6.03%的物体幻觉。该方法在保持最先进性能的同时，仅增加少量推理成本，并保留了其他基础能力。

Conclusion: CAST是一种有效的幻觉缓解方法，通过基于标题的视觉注意力引导来增强LVLMs的视觉感知能力，在减少物体幻觉方面表现优异，同时保持了方法的简单性和低计算成本。

Abstract: Although Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance on downstream tasks, they frequently produce contents that deviate from visual information, leading to object hallucination. To tackle this, recent works mostly depend on expensive manual annotations and training cost, or decoding strategies which significantly increase inference time. In this work, we observe that LVLMs' attention to visual information is significantly enhanced when answering caption queries compared to non-caption queries. Inspired by this phenomenon, we propose Caption-guided Visual Attention Steering (CAST), a training-free, plug-and-play hallucination mitigation method that leverages the attention activation pattern corresponding to caption queries to enhance LVLMs' visual perception capability. Specifically, we use probing techniques to identify attention heads that are highly sensitive to caption queries and estimate optimized steering directions for their outputs. This steering strengthens LVLM's fine-grained visual perception capabilities, thereby effectively mitigating object hallucination. CAST reduced object hallucination by an average of 6.03% across five widely used LVLMs and five benchmarks including both discriminative and generative tasks, demonstrating state-of-the-art performance while adding little inference cost and preserving other foundational capabilities.

</details>


### [43] [Contact Matrix: Enhancing Dance Motion Synthesis with Precise Interaction Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04662)
*Xuhai Chen,Zhi Cen,Huaijin Pi,Sida Peng,Xiaowei Zhou,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段框架来生成逼真的双人舞反应动作，通过运动VQ-VAE和接触感知扩散模型，显著提升了互动保真度和节奏同步性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成逼真的反应性动作具有挑战性，特别是在双人舞场景中，存在严格的互动约束和有限的可行解空间。高质量数据稀缺、运动模式复杂、人际互动细节丰富且微妙，这些因素使得现有方法难以生成高质量的双人互动动作。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：1）第一阶段采用运动VQ-VAE，包含独立的身体部位编码器和联合解码器，使用专门的码书增强表示能力，同时在解码过程中动态建模身体部位间的依赖关系；2）第二阶段提出接触感知扩散模型，联合生成动作和个体间的接触矩阵，实现明确的互动建模，并在采样过程中提供更精确和约束的互动动态指导。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在双人舞场景中优于Duolando方法，具有更低的FID_k（8.89 vs. 25.30）和FID_cd（8.01 vs. 9.97），以及更高的BED（0.4606 vs. 0.2858），表明在互动保真度和节奏同步性方面有明显改进。

Conclusion: 本文提出的两阶段框架通过创新的运动VQ-VAE设计和接触感知扩散模型，有效解决了双人舞反应动作生成中的互动建模难题，在保证动作一致性的同时显著提升了互动质量和节奏同步性，为复杂人际互动动作生成提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Generating realistic reactive motions, in which one person reacts to the fixed motions of others, is challenging due to strict interaction constraints and a limited feasible solution space. This paper focuses on a typical scenario: duet dance, where high-quality data is scarce, motion patterns are complex, and the details of human interactions are both intricate and abundant. To tackle these challenges, we propose a novel two-stage framework. In the first stage, we introduce a motion VQ-VAE with separate body-part encoders and a joint decoder, enabling specialized codebooks to enhance representation capacity while dynamically modeling dependencies across body parts during decoding, thereby preventing inconsistencies in the generated motions. In the second stage, we propose a contact-aware diffusion model for reactive motion generation that jointly generates motion and a contact matrix between individuals, enabling explicit interaction modeling and providing guidance toward more precise and constrained interaction dynamics during sampling. Experiments show that our method outperforms Duolando with lower $\text{FID}_k$ (8.89 vs. 25.30) and $\text{FID}_{cd}$ (8.01 vs. 9.97), as well as a higher BED (0.4606 vs. 0.2858), indicating improved interaction fidelity and rhythmic synchronization.

</details>


### [44] [Physical Adversarial Clothing Evades Visible-Thermal Detectors via Non-Overlapping RGB-T Pattern](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04675)
*Xiaopei Zhu,Guanning Zeng,Zhanhao Hu,Jun Zhu,Xiaolin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种针对可见光-热红外（RGB-T）物体检测器的物理攻击方法，使用非重叠的RGB-T对抗图案（NORP），通过空间离散连续优化（SDCO）方法优化，实现了对多种融合架构检测器的高效攻击


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见光-热红外物体检测在自动驾驶等应用中至关重要，但目前RGB-T检测器的安全性在物理世界中被忽视，缺乏针对多模态融合系统的物理对抗攻击研究

Method: 提出NORP对抗图案设计（可见光和热红外材料不重叠），采用SDCO空间离散连续优化方法优化对抗服装，构建3D RGB-T模型模拟全方位攻击，并提出融合阶段集成方法增强对抗样本的迁移性

Result: 在数字和物理世界中均实现了对多种RGB-T融合架构检测器的高攻击成功率，提出的融合阶段集成方法显著提升了对抗攻击在未见检测器上的迁移性

Conclusion: NORP对抗图案和SDCO优化方法是有效的RGB-T物理攻击方案，揭示了RGB-T检测系统在物理世界中的安全漏洞，为未来安全防护研究提供了基础

Abstract: Visible-thermal (RGB-T) object detection is a crucial technology for applications such as autonomous driving, where multimodal fusion enhances performance in challenging conditions like low light. However, the security of RGB-T detectors, particularly in the physical world, has been largely overlooked. This paper proposes a novel approach to RGB-T physical attacks using adversarial clothing with a non-overlapping RGB-T pattern (NORP). To simulate full-view (0$^{\circ}$--360$^{\circ}$) RGB-T attacks, we construct 3D RGB-T models for human and adversarial clothing. NORP is a new adversarial pattern design using distinct visible and thermal materials without overlap, avoiding the light reduction in overlapping RGB-T patterns (ORP). To optimize the NORP on adversarial clothing, we propose a spatial discrete-continuous optimization (SDCO) method. We systematically evaluated our method on RGB-T detectors with different fusion architectures, demonstrating high attack success rates both in the digital and physical worlds. Additionally, we introduce a fusion-stage ensemble method that enhances the transferability of adversarial attacks across unseen RGB-T detectors with different fusion architectures.

</details>


### [45] [Multi-Level Bidirectional Biomimetic Learning for EEG-Based Visual Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04680)
*Jingtao Liu,Peiliang Gong,Chuhang Zheng,Yiheng Liu,Qi Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了MB2L框架，通过整合生理学先验知识解决EEG-视觉跨模态对齐难题，显著提升了零样本EEG到图像检索的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基视觉神经解码面临两大挑战：1) 配对数据有限；2) 高保真数字图像与生物视觉感知之间存在根本性失配，这种失配源于视网膜拓扑映射和受试者特异性神经解剖结构带来的感知扭曲。

Method: 提出MB2L框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 基于视觉先验的自适应模糊方法，根据视网膜拓扑先验重加权视觉输入以缓解感知-结构失配；2) 仿生视觉特征提取模块，学习与分层皮层处理一致的多层次视觉表征，增强受试者不变编码；3) 多层次双向对比学习，通过双向对比目标在共享语义空间中对齐EEG和视觉特征。

Result: 在零样本EEG到图像检索任务上，MB2L取得了80.5%的Top-1准确率和97.6%的Top-5准确率，显著优于现有方法，并在不同受试者和实验设置下展现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: MB2L框架通过将结构化的生理学归纳偏置整合到表征学习中，有效解决了EEG-视觉跨模态对齐的关键挑战，为脑机接口和神经解码应用提供了有力工具。

Abstract: EEG-based visual neural decoding aims to align neural responses with visual stimuli for tasks such as image retrieval. However, limited paired data and a fundamental mismatch between high-fidelity digital images and biological visual perception - distorted by retinotopic mapping and subject-specific neuroanatomy - severely impede cross-modal alignment. To address this, we propose MB2L, a Multi-Level Bidirectional Biomimetic Learning framework that incorporates structured physiological inductive biases into representation learning. Specifically, we propose Adaptive Blur with Visual Priors to mitigate perceptual-structural mismatch by reweighting visual inputs according to retinotopic priors. We further propose Biomimetic Visual Feature Extraction to learn multi-level visual representations consistent with hierarchical cortical processing, enhancing subject-invariant encoding. These modules are jointly optimized via Multi-level Bidirectional Contrastive Learning, which aligns EEG and visual features in a shared semantic space through bidirectional contrastive objectives. Experiments show MB2L achieves 80.5% Top-1 and 97.6% Top-5 accuracy on zero-shot EEG-to-image retrieval, significantly outperforming prior methods and demonstrating strong generalization across subjects and experimental settings.

</details>


### [46] [FaithfulFaces: Pose-Faithful Facial Identity Preservation for Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04702)
*Yuanzhi Wang,Xuhua Ren,Jiaxiang Cheng,Bing Ma,Kai Yu,Sen Liang,Wenyue Li,Tianxiang Zheng,Qinglin Lu,Zhen Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FaithfulFaces是一个用于文本到视频生成的身份保持框架，通过姿态共享身份对齐器和姿态变化-身份不变性约束，在复杂动态场景中保持面部身份的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的身份保持文本到视频生成方法在大姿态变化或面部遮挡时存在显著的身份失真问题，需要改进在复杂动态场景中的身份保持能力。

Method: 提出了姿态共享身份对齐器，通过姿态共享字典和姿态变化-身份不变性约束来精炼和跨不同视角对齐面部姿态；将单视角输入映射到具有显式欧拉角嵌入的全局面部姿态表示；创建了包含大量面部姿态多样性的高质量视频数据集。

Result: FaithfulFaces实现了最先进的性能，即使在姿态变化和遮挡发生时也能保持优越的身份一致性和结构清晰度。

Conclusion: FaithfulFaces框架通过姿态忠实的面部先验引导生成基础模型，实现了在复杂动态场景中鲁棒的身份保持视频生成。

Abstract: Identity-preserving text-to-video generation (IPT2V) empowers users to produce diverse and imaginative videos with consistent human facial identity. Despite recent progress, existing methods often suffer from significant identity distortion under large facial pose variations or facial occlusions. In this paper, we propose \textit{FaithfulFaces}, a pose-faithful facial identity preservation learning framework to improve IPT2V in complex dynamic scenes. The key of FaithfulFaces is a pose-shared identity aligner that refines and aligns facial poses across distinct views via a pose-shared dictionary and a pose variation-identity invariance constraint. By mapping single-view inputs into a global facial pose representation with explicit Euler angle embeddings, FaithfulFaces provides a pose-faithful facial prior that guides generative foundations toward robust identity-preserving generation. In particular, we develop a specialized pipeline to curate a high-quality video dataset featuring substantial facial pose diversity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FaithfulFaces achieves state-of-the-art performance, maintaining superior identity consistency and structural clarity even as pose changes and occlusions occur.

</details>


### [47] [Anny-Fit: All-Age Human Mesh Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04728)
*Laura Bravo-Sánchez,Matthieu Armando,Romain Brégier,Grégory Rogez,Serena Yeung-Levy,Fabien Baradel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Anny-Fit：一种面向全年龄段的多人3D人体网格恢复优化框架，通过联合优化相机坐标系中所有个体并利用多源专家知识（深度图、分割、关键点、VLM语义属性），解决全年龄段场景中的深度尺度歧义问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单图像3D人体姿态和形状恢复方法通常假设成人主体并独立优化每个人，无法处理真实世界中包含各年龄段人群的场景，其中人体比例和深度需要联合解析。

Method: 1. 提出多人在相机空间联合优化的框架，强制全局空间一致性 2. 整合多种专家知识：度量深度图、实例分割、2D关键点、VLM衍生的年龄/性别等语义属性 3. 通过伪标注将VLM语义知识蒸馏到HMR模型中 4. 实现成人训练HMR流水线到全年龄段的零样本适应

Result: 在多个数据集上显著改进：2D重投影精度提升13-16，相对深度排序提升6-7，3D估计误差降低9-29，形状估计提升25-82，生成更连贯的场景。同时能够学习语义有意义的形状参数并提升HMR性能。

Conclusion: Anny-Fit通过联合优化和整合多源专家知识有效解决了全年龄段场景中的深度尺度歧义问题，桥接了成人专用模型与全年龄建模，实现了无需重新训练的零样本适应。

Abstract: Recovering 3D human pose and shape from a single image remains a cornerstone of human-centric vision, yet most methods assume adult subjects and optimize each person independently. These assumptions fail in real-world, all-age scenes, where body proportions and depth must be resolved jointly. We introduce Anny-Fit, a multi-person, camera-space optimization framework for all-age 3D human mesh recovery (HMR). Unlike existing per-person fitting methods, Anny-Fit jointly optimizes all individuals directly in the camera coordinate system, enforcing global spatial consistency. At the core of our approach is the use of multiple forms of expert knowledge -- including metric depth maps, instance segmentation, 2D keypoints, and, VLM-derived semantic attributes such as age and gender -- each obtained from dedicated off-the-shelf networks. These complementary signals jointly guide the optimization, constraining the depth-scale ambiguity characteristic of all-age scenes. Across diverse datasets, Anny-Fit consistently improves 2D reprojection accuracy (+13 to 16), relative depth ordering (+6 to 7), 3D estimation error (-9 to -29) and shape estimation (+25 to +82), producing more coherent scenes. Finally, we show that VLM-based semantic knowledge can be distilled into an HMR model via the pseudo-ground-truth annotations produced by Anny-Fit on training data, enabling it to learn semantically meaningful shape parameters while improving HMR performance. Our approach bridges adult-only and all-age modeling by enabling zero-shot adaptation of adult-trained HMR pipelines to the full age spectrum without retraining. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/naver/anny-fit.

</details>


### [48] [ULF-Loc: Unbiased Landmark Feature for Robust Visual Localization with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04730)
*Yingdong Gu,Shaocheng Yan,Zhenjun Zhao,Yuan Kou,Jianxin Luo,Pengcheng Shi,Jiayuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ULF-Loc：基于无偏地标特征的视觉定位方法，通过几何加权特征融合解决3D高斯特征偏差问题，在剑桥地标数据集上实现了17%的平均中值平移误差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)的视觉定位方法存在特征偏差问题，因为广泛采用的α混合优化会使3D点特征产生固有偏差，这源于单个高斯与其相邻高斯的纠缠，导致学习到的特征不适合精确匹配任务。

Method: 1) 理论上分析了3DGS中的特征学习过程，揭示了α混合优化引入偏差的机制；2) 提出无偏地标特征框架(ULF-Loc)，用几何加权特征融合替代有偏的特征优化；3) 引入关键点共识地标采样选择可靠高斯；4) 使用局部几何一致性验证来拒绝由渲染伪影引起的误匹配。

Result: 在剑桥地标数据集上，ULF-Loc比最先进的方法(STDLoc)减少了17%的平均中值平移误差，同时训练时间仅为1/10，GPU内存使用仅为1/6，实现了卓越的效率。

Conclusion: ULF-Loc通过解决3D高斯特征中的固有偏差问题，显著提高了视觉定位的精度和效率，为增强现实和自主导航应用提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Visual localization is a core technology for augmented reality and autonomous navigation. Recent methods combine the efficient rendering of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) with feature-based localization. These methods rely on direct matching between 2D query features and the 3D Gaussian feature field, but this often results in mismatches due to an inherent bias in the learned Gaussian feature. We theoretically analyze the feature learning process in 3DGS, revealing that the widely adopted $α$-blending optimization inherently introduces bias into 3D point features. This bias stems from the entanglement between individual Gaussians and their neighboring Gaussians, making the learned features unsuitable for precise matching tasks. Motivated by these findings, we propose ULF-Loc, an unbiased landmark feature framework that replaces biased feature optimization with geometry-weighted feature fusion. We further introduce keypoint-consensus landmark sampling to select reliable Gaussians and local geometric consistency verification to reject mismatches caused by rendering artifacts. On the Cambridge Landmarks dataset, ULF-Loc reduces the mean median translation error by 17\% compared to the state-of-the-art, while achieving superior efficiency with only 1/10 the training time and 1/6 the GPU memory of STDLoc.

</details>


### [49] [VC-FeS: Viewpoint-Conditioned Feature Selection for Vehicle Re-identification in Thermal Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04750)
*Yasod Ginige,Ransika Gunasekara,Darsha Hewavitharana,Manjula Ariyarathne,Peshala Jayasekara,Ranga Rodrigo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种用于热图像中低纹理物体识别的视角条件特征提取方法，通过构建视角条件特征向量和区域特定特征比较，显著提升现有RGB预训练ViT模型在热图像域的识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热图像等单通道图像中的低纹理物体识别在监控等应用中很重要。现有方法性能较差，原因是：1）缺少颜色信息时同类物体形状相似度高；2）纹理信息弱化；3）视角变化导致特征变化。这些问题在热图像领域尤其突出。

Method: 构建视角条件特征向量和区域特定特征比较在独立特征空间中实现。通过这两项干预措施，有效利用现有RGB预训练ViT特征提取器的优势，同时专门解决热图像领域的挑战。

Result: 在RGBNT100（红外）车辆数据集和自采集的热图像海事数据集上测试。mAP分数分别超过最先进方法19.7%和12.8%。计划发布首个用于海事船只识别的热图像数据集。

Conclusion: 提出的视角条件特征提取方法成功解决了热图像中低纹理物体识别的挑战，显著提升了识别性能，并为该领域贡献了首个海事热图像数据集。

Abstract: Identification of less-articulated objects using single-channel images, such as thermal images, is important in many applications, such as surveillance. However, in this domain, existing methods show poor performance due to high similarity among objects of the same category in the absence of color information (overlooking shape information) and de-emphasized texture information. Furthermore, variability in viewpoint adds more complexity as the features vary from side to side. We address these issues by constructing viewpoint-conditioned feature vectors and area-specific feature comparisons in separate feature spaces. These interventions enable leveraging the advancements of existing RGB-pre-trained ViT feature extractors while effectively adapting them to address the challenges specific to the thermal domain. We test our system with RGBNT100 (IR) vehicle dataset and a thermal maritime dataset acquired by us. Our results surpass the state-of-the-art methods by 19.7% and 12.8% for the above datasets in mAP scores, respectively. We also plan to make our thermal dataset available, the first of its kind for maritime vessel identification.

</details>


### [50] [FairEnc: A Fair Vision-Language Model with Fair Vision and Text Encoders for Glaucoma Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04882)
*Mohamed Elhabebe,Ayman El-Baz,Qing Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FairEnc是一种公平预训练方法，用于视觉语言模型，能够同时跨多个敏感属性进行去偏，在青光眼检测任务中降低人口统计学差异同时保持诊断性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化青光眼检测对预防不可逆视力损失至关重要，但确保跨不同患者群体的公平性仍然是一个重大挑战。需要解决视觉语言模型中跨多种敏感属性（种族、性别、民族、语言）的偏见问题。

Method: 提出FairEnc公平预训练方法：1)文本编码器：利用大语言模型生成包含不同敏感属性的合成临床描述，同时保持疾病语义，采用对比对齐目标鼓励人口统计学不变的表示；2)视觉编码器：提出双级公平策略，结合互信息正则化以减少学习特征与人口统计学群体之间的统计依赖性，并使用多判别器对抗去偏。

Result: 在公开的Harvard-FairVLMed数据集上，FairEnc有效降低了DPD和DEOdds衡量的群体差异，同时在零样本和线性探测评估中实现了强诊断性能。在私有FairFundus数据集上，FairEnc在跨域和跨模态设置下保持公平性优势，并在竞争范围内保持诊断性能。

Conclusion: FairEnc能够增强视觉语言模型的公平性，降低多敏感属性偏见，在分布偏移下保持公平泛化能力，支持其在真实世界临床环境中更公平部署的潜力。

Abstract: Automated glaucoma detection is critical for preventing irreversible vision loss and reducing the burden on healthcare systems. However, ensuring fairness across diverse patient populations remains a significant challenge. In this paper, we propose FairEnc, a fair pretraining method for vision-language models (VLMs) that enables simultaneous debiasing across multiple sensitive attributes. FairEnc jointly mitigates biases in both textual and visual modalities with respect to multiple sensitive attributes, including race, gender, ethnicity, and language. Specifically, for the textual encoder, we leverage a large language model to generate synthetic clinical descriptions with varied sensitive attributes while preserving disease semantics, and employ a contrastive alignment objective to encourage demographic-invariant representations. For the visual encoder, we propose a dual-level fairness strategy that combines mutual information regularization to reduce statistical dependence between learned features and demographic groups, with multi-discriminator adversarial debiasing. Comprehensive experiments on the publicly available Harvard-FairVLMed dataset demonstrate that FairEnc effectively reduces demographic disparity as measured by DPD and DEOdds while achieving strong diagnostic performance under both zero-shot and linear probing evaluations. Additional experiments on the private FairFundus dataset show that FairEnc consistently preserves fairness advantages under cross-domain and cross-modality settings and maintains diagnostic performance within a competitive range. These results highlight FairEnc's ability to generalize fairness under distribution shifts, supporting its potential for more equitable deployment in real-world clinical settings. Our codebase and synthetic clinical notes are available at https://github.com/Mohamed-Elhabebe/FairEnc

</details>


### [51] [When Relations Break: Analyzing Relation Hallucination in Vision-Language Model Under Rotation and Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05045)
*Philip Wootaek Shin,Ajay Narayanan Sridhar,Sivani Devarapalli,Rui Zhang,Jack Sampson,Vijaykrishnan Narayanan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究发现即使轻微的视觉扰动（旋转和噪声）也会显著降低视觉语言模型的关系推理能力，现有缓解策略只能部分改善，揭示了感知鲁棒性与关系理解之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在多模态任务中表现出色，但仍然容易出现关系幻觉问题，特别是在需要准确推理物体间交互关系时。研究者希望探究视觉扰动对关系推理的影响程度。

Method: 通过引入两种视觉扰动（旋转和噪声），在不同模型和数据集上评估关系推理性能的退化程度。进一步评估了基于提示的增强和预处理策略（方向校正和去噪）的改善效果。

Result: 结果显示：1) 即使轻度失真也会显著降低关系推理能力；2) 提示增强和预处理策略只能提供部分改善，无法完全消除幻觉；3) 在视觉扰动下，模型的关系理解存在系统性脆弱性。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了感知鲁棒性与关系理解之间的差距，强调了开发更具鲁棒性、具备几何感知能力的视觉语言模型的必要性，以更好地处理物体间交互推理任务。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong multimodal performance but remain prone to relation hallucination, which requires accurate reasoning over inter-object interactions. We study the impact of visual perturbations, specifically rotation and noise, and show that even mild distortions significantly degrade relational reasoning across models and datasets. We further evaluate prompt-based augmentation and preprocessing strategies (orientation correction and denoising), finding that while they offer partial improvements, they do not fully resolve hallucinations. Our results reveal a gap between perceptual robustness and relational understanding, highlighting the need for more robust, geometry-aware VLMs.

</details>


### [52] [Lightweight Cross-Spectral Face Recognition via Contrastive Alignment and Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04769)
*Anjith George,Sebastien Marcel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的异构人脸识别框架，通过适配混合CNN-Transformer模型，实现高效端到端训练，在保持低计算要求的同时在异构和同构人脸识别任务上达到竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构人脸识别（HFR）系统在跨模态匹配（如热成像到可见光）方面面临实际应用需求，但现有方法大多依赖计算密集型模型，难以在资源受限的边缘设备上部署。需要开发既能保持高性能又具有低计算开销的轻量化解决方案。

Method: 作者引入了一个轻量级但有效的HFR框架，通过适配原本为RGB同构人脸识别设计的混合CNN-Transformer模型。该方法支持使用少量配对异构数据进行高效的端到端训练，同时仍能在标准RGB人脸识别基准上保持强大性能，使其同时适用于同构和异构场景。

Result: 在多个挑战性的异构人脸识别和人脸识别基准上的综合实验表明，该方法在保持低计算需求的同时，达到了最先进或具有竞争力的性能水平。

Conclusion: 提出的轻量级异构人脸识别框架通过混合CNN-Transformer模型的有效适配，为资源受限的边缘设备提供了实用的解决方案，在计算效率和识别性能之间取得了良好平衡，既适用于异构场景也兼容同构场景。

Abstract: Heterogeneous Face Recognition (HFR) aims at matching face images captured across different sensing modalities, such as thermal-to-visible or near-infrared-to-visible, enhancing the usability of face recognition systems in challenging real-world conditions. Although recent HFR methods have achieved significant improvements in performance, many rely on computationally expensive models, making them impractical for deployment on resource-limited edge devices. In this work, we introduce a lightweight yet effective HFR framework by adapting a hybrid CNN-Transformer model originally developed for RGB homogeneous face recognition. Our approach enables efficient end-to-end training with only a small amount of paired heterogeneous data, while still maintaining strong performance on standard RGB face recognition benchmarks. This makes it suitable for both homogeneous and heterogeneous settings. Comprehensive experiments on several challenging HFR and face recognition benchmarks show that our method achieves state-of-the-art or competitive performance while keeping computational requirements low.

</details>


### [53] [DART: A Vision-Language Foundation Model for Comprehensive Rope Condition Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04943)
*Anju Rani,Daniel Ortiz-Arroyo,Petar Durdevic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DART是一个用于合成纤维绳损伤评估的视觉-语言基础模型，通过统一的多任务架构处理完整的绳索检查工作流，包括损伤分类、连续严重度估计、异常标记和报告生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成纤维绳在海洋、海上和工业应用中的状态监测需要超越传统分类器的解决方案。检查员需要从单张检查图像中获得连续严重度估计、维护建议、异常标志、劣化时间线和自动化报告等完整信息。

Method: 提出DART模型，将联合嵌入预测架构扩展到跨模态领域，耦合Vision Transformer和Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct模型。创新包括：HD-MASK（显著性引导的掩码策略）、每类别可学习的严重度门控、对比损伤解纠缠损失，通过严重度调节的跨模态融合模块实现多任务处理。

Result: 在14个细粒度绳索损伤类别的4,270张图像上训练后，冻结的DART骨干无需任务特定微调即可支持下游任务：损伤分类（93.22%准确率，91.04%宏观F1，比仅视觉基线提升38.5个百分点），连续严重度回归（Spearman rho = 0.94，1序数内准确率99.6%），少量样本识别（20个样本下89.2%宏观F1）。

Conclusion: DART作为一个通用状态监测骨干模型，超越了传统分类任务，从单一共享表示中提供可操作的检查智能，为合成纤维绳的全面损伤评估提供了端到端解决方案。

Abstract: The condition monitoring (CM) of synthetic fibre ropes (SFRs) used in offshore, maritime, and industrial settings demands more than a classifier: inspectors need continuous severity estimates, maintenance recommendations, anomaly flags, deterioration timelines, and automated reports, all from a single inspection image. We present DART (Damage Assessment via Rope Transformer), a vision-language foundation model that addresses the full rope inspection workflow through a unified multi-task architecture. DART extends the Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) to the cross-modal domain by coupling a Vision Transformer (ViT-H/14) with Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct via a Severity-Conditioned Cross-Modal Fusion (SC-CMF) module. Three architectural innovations drive the model's versatility: (1) HD-MASK, a saliency-guided masking strategy that focuses self-supervised reconstruction on damage-dense patches; (2) per-class learnable severity gates that adaptively weight language grounding by damage category; and (3) a Contrastive Damage Disentanglement (CDD) loss that shapes the embedding space to simultaneously encode damage type, severity ordering, and cross-modal semantics. Trained once on 4,270 images spanning 14 fine-grained rope damage classes, the frozen DART backbone supports downstream tasks without any task-specific fine-tuning: damage classification (93.22 % accuracy, 91.04 % macro-F1, +38.5 pp over a vision-only baseline), continuous severity regression (Spearman rho = 0.94, within-1-ordinal accuracy 99.6 %), few-shot recognition (89.2 % macro-F1 at 20 shots). These results demonstrate that DART functions as a general-purpose CM backbone that goes well beyond classification, providing actionable inspection intelligence from a single shared representation.

</details>


### [54] [Gaze4HRI: Zero-shot Benchmarking Gaze Estimation Neural-Networks for Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04770)
*Berk Sezer,Ali Görkem Küçük,Erol Şahin,Sinan Kalkan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Gaze4HRI数据集和基准，用于评估零样本外观基于3D视线估计在HRI环境下的鲁棒性，发现数据多样性比复杂模型架构更重要


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本外观基于3D视线估计方法在HRI环境下的可靠性不确定，现有基准缺乏对动态摄像机视角、移动目标等HRI基本条件的考虑，且交叉数据集评估存在复杂度差距问题

Method: 构建了Gaze4HRI大规模数据集（50+被试，3,000+视频，600,000+帧），设计了专门评估HRI关键变量的基准：光照、头-视线冲突、摄像机运动和视线目标运动

Result: 所有评估方法至少在一个条件下失效，特别是向下看是一个普遍失效点；PureGaze在ETH-X-Gaze数据集上训练后，在其他所有条件中保持鲁棒性；数据多样性是零样本鲁棒性的主要驱动力

Conclusion: 研究挑战了当前文献中对复杂时空建模和Transformer架构的关注，表明大规模数据多样性（如ETH-X-Gaze）是实现无约束环境下零样本鲁棒性的关键，而像PureGaze的自对抗损失这样的鲁棒性增强框架能提供进一步改进

Abstract: While zero-shot appearance-based 3D gaze estimation offers significant cost-efficiency by directly mapping RGB images to gaze vectors, its reliability in Human-Robot Interaction (HRI) settings remains uncertain. Existing benchmarks frequently overlook fundamental HRI conditions, such as dynamic camera viewpoints and moving targets in video. Furthermore, current cross-dataset evaluations often suffer from a complexity gap, where methods trained on diverse datasets are tested on significantly smaller and less varied sets, failing to assess true robustness. To bridge these gaps, we introduce Gaze4HRI, a large-scale dataset (50+ subjects, 3,000+ videos, 600,000+ frames) designed to evaluate state-of-the-art performance against critical HRI variables: illumination, head-gaze conflict, as well as the motion of camera and gaze target in video. Our benchmark reveals that all evaluated methods fail in at least one condition, identifying steeply-downward gaze as a universal failure point. Notably, PureGaze trained on the ETH-X-Gaze dataset uniquely maintains resilience across all other conditions. These results challenge the recent focus in the literature on complex spatial-temporal modeling and Transformer-based architectures. Instead, our findings suggest that extensive data diversity, as exemplified by the ETH-X-Gaze dataset, serves as the primary driver of zero-shot robustness in unconstrained environments, while resilience-enhancing frameworks, such as PureGaze's self-adversarial loss for gaze feature purification, provide a substantial further improvement. Ultimately, this study establishes a rigorous benchmark that provides practical guidelines for practitioners as well as reshaping future research. The dataset and codes are available at https://gazeforhri.github.io.

</details>


### [55] [MIRAGE: Retrieval and Generation of Multimodal Images and Texts for Medical Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04772)
*Miguel Diaz Benito,Cecilia Diana Albelda,Alvaro Garcia Martin,Jesus Bescos Cano,Marcos Escudero-Vinolo,Juan C. SanMiguel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIRAGE是一个多模态医学文本和图像检索与生成系统，它允许用户通过文本或图像查询来检索和生成临床相关的医学图像，并提供丰富的医学描述。该系统基于微调的医学版CLIP模型，集成了医学扩散模型和大语言模型，旨在为医学学生提供免费的交互式教学工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学教学面临两个主要问题：一是传统医学图谱体积大且缺乏交互性，二是在线医学图像搜索经常出现标签错误或不完整的材料。医学学生需要一个能够从可信来源获取和生成临床相关图像的系统，以提高诊断技能和解剖结构理解。

Method: 基于微调的医学版CLIP模型(MedICaT-ROCO)，在ROCO数据集上进行训练。系统包含三个核心组件：1) 多模态检索模块，将文本和图像映射到共享潜在空间进行语义查询；2) 医学扩散模型(Prompt2MedImage)用于生成合成图像；3) 大语言模型(Dolly-v2-3b)提供丰富描述。系统还支持双搜索选项，用于不同医学状况的视觉比较。

Result: 开发了MIRAGE系统，该系统完全基于公开可用的预训练模型构建，确保可复现性和可访问性。系统具有交互式界面，支持医学图像检索、合成生成和丰富描述功能。系统已部署在Kaggle平台上，全球医学学生无需本地计算资源或技术专业知识即可访问使用。

Conclusion: MIRAGE为医学教育提供了一个免费、透明且易用的教学工具，特别适合没有编程技能的医学学生。该系统通过交互式和个性化的视觉学习体验，帮助医学学生提高诊断能力和医学理解，解决了传统医学教学资源的局限性问题。

Abstract: Access to diverse, well-annotated medical images with interactive learning tools is fundamental for training practitioners in medicine and related fields to improve their diagnostic skills and understanding of anatomical structures. While medical atlases are valuable, they are often impractical due to their size and lack of interactivity, whereas online image search may provide mislabeled or incomplete material. To address this, we propose MIRAGE, a multimodal medical text and image retrieval and generation system that allows users to find and generate clinically relevant images from trustworthy sources by mapping both text and images to a shared latent space, enabling semantically meaningful queries. The system is based on a fine-tuned medical version of CLIP (MedICaT-ROCO), trained with the ROCO dataset, obtained from PubMed Central. MIRAGE allows users to give prompts to retrieve images, generate synthetic ones through a medical diffusion model (Prompt2MedImage) and receive enriched descriptions from a large language model (Dolly-v2-3b). It also supports a dual search option, enabling the visual comparison of different medical conditions. A key advantage of the system is that it relies entirely on publicly available pretrained models, ensuring reproducibility and accessibility. Our goal is to provide a free, transparent and easy-to-use didactic tool for medical students, especially those without programming skills. The system features an interface that enables interactive and personalized visual learning through medical image retrieval and generation. The system is accessible to medical students worldwide without requiring local computational resources or technical expertise, and is currently deployed on Kaggle: http://www-vpu.eps.uam.es/mirage

</details>


### [56] [Local Intrinsic Dimension Unveils Hallucinations in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05026)
*Bartlomiej Sobieski,Matthew Tivnan,Dawid Płudowski,Michał Jan Włodarczyk,Pengfei Jin,Przemyslaw Biecek,Quanzheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出将扩散模型中的结构幻觉视为模型诱导流形上的不稳定性，通过局部固有维度分析识别其主要驱动力，并提出名为"固有淬灭"的直接校正机制来减少幻觉生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型容易生成结构幻觉，即样本符合训练数据的统计特性但违反底层结构规则（如生成超过五个手指的手）。现有研究从模式插值等角度提供了部分解释，但需要补充视角来全面理解这一问题。

Method: 论文提出将幻觉视为模型诱导流形上的不稳定性，基于此构建幻觉过滤器。通过追踪不稳定性来源，识别局部固有维度为主要驱动力，并提出"固有淬灭"方法，通过压缩局部固有维度来缓解幻觉。

Result: 基于不稳定性的幻觉过滤器性能达到或超过最近提出的时间过滤器。固有淬灭方法在多个基准测试中持续优于标准幻觉减少基线，特别是在下游医学成像任务中为强制解剖一致性提供了有前景的解决方案。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了理解扩散模型结构幻觉的新视角，提出的固有淬灭方法能有效减少幻觉生成，特别是在需要精确解剖结构的医学成像等应用领域具有重要价值。

Abstract: Diffusion models are prone to generating structural hallucinations - samples that match the statistical properties of the training data yet defy underlying structural rules, resulting in anomalies like hands with more than five fingers. Recent research studied this failure mode from several viewpoints, offering partial explanations to their occurrence, such as mode interpolation. In this work, we propose a complementary perspective that treats hallucinations as instabilities on the model-induced manifold. We begin by showing that a hallucination filter based on such instabilities matches or exceeds the performance of the recently proposed temporal one. By tracing the source of these instabilities, we identify local intrinsic dimension (LID) as their primary driver and propose Intrinsic Quenching (IQ), a direct corrective mechanism that deflates it to alleviate hallucinations. IQ consistently outperforms standard hallucination reduction baselines across a wide array of benchmarks and offers a highly promising solution for enforcing anatomical consistency in downstream medical imaging tasks.

</details>


### [57] [QuadBox: Accelerating 3D Gaussian Splatting with Geometry-Aware Boxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04844)
*Xinze Li,Bohan Yang,Pengxu Chen,Yiyuan Wang,Hongcheng Luo,Wentao Cheng,Weifeng Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QuadBox是一种优化3D高斯泼溅渲染的方法，通过使用四个轴对齐边界框来紧密封装投影高斯分布，配合单通瓦片遍历算法QPass，将3DGS渲染速度提升1.85倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)虽能实现实时新颖视角合成，但其渲染流水线中精确计算高斯-瓦片交集的效率仍然是关键瓶颈。现有方法在高斯与瓦片交集计算上不够高效，需要更优化的算法来提升整体渲染性能。

Method: 提出QuadBox方法，包含两个核心组件：1) 几何感知拉伸因子，用于构建瓦片对齐的QuadBox，紧密覆盖椭圆投影并排除无关瓦片；2) QPass单通瓦片遍历算法，充分利用QuadBox的离散特性，通过简单的区间测试完成瓦片交集检查。

Result: 在公开数据集上的实验表明，QuadBox方法将3DGS的渲染速度提升了1.85倍，显著加速了渲染过程，同时保持了渲染质量。

Conclusion: QuadBox通过离散化边界框表示和高效瓦片遍历算法，为3D高斯泼溅提供了有效的渲染优化方案，在保持渲染质量的同时实现了显著的性能提升，代码已开源。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as an advanced technique for real-time novel view synthesis by representing scene geometry and appearance using differentiable Gaussian primitives. However, efficiently computing precise Gaussian-tile intersections remains a critical task in the rasterization pipeline. To this end, we propose QuadBox, a method that leverages four axis-aligned bounding boxes to tightly encapsulate projected Gaussians in a discrete manner. First, we derive a geometry-aware stretching factor that enables the construction of a tile-aligned QuadBox, which covers the elliptical projection and largely excludes irrelevant tiles. Second, we introduce QPass, a single-pass tile traversal algorithm that exhaustively exploits the discrete nature of QuadBox, ensuring that the tile intersection check is performed with simple interval tests. Experiments on public datasets show that our method accelerates the rendering speed of 3DGS by 1.85$\times$. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/Powertony102/QuadBox}{https://github.com/Powertony102/QuadBox}.

</details>


### [58] [Direct Product Flow Matching: Decoupling Radial and Angular Dynamics for Few-Shot Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05054)
*Hongxu Chen,Yanghao Wang,Bowei Zhu,Hongxiang Li,Zhen Wang,Ziqi Jiang,Lin Li,Rui Liu,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了Warped Product Flow Matching (WP-FM)框架，通过解耦圆柱流形上的径向和角度演化，解决了现有流匹配方法在几何先验不兼容和数据集特定信息丢失的问题，在少样本适应任务中取得了新的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配方法在视觉语言模型的少样本适应中存在三个关键问题：1) 角度动态扭曲 - 径向角度耦合导致角度子流形上的非均匀速度；2) 径向动态忽略 - 特征归一化丢弃了模态置信度，无法区分分布内外数据；3) 上下文无关的无条件流 - 预训练跨模态特征提取过程中的数据集特定信息丢失无法恢复。这些问题源于预训练跨模态特征的几何先验不兼容。

Method: 提出了统一黎曼框架Warped Product Flow Matching (WP-FM)，在扭曲乘积流形上重新制定对齐。在此基础上推导出Direct Product Flow Matching (DP-FM)，通过引入恒定扭曲度量产生解耦的圆柱流形（即直积流形）。DP-FM实现了独立的径向演化和恒定速度的角度测地传输，同时通过将流条件化于预训练VLM的隐藏状态来融入分类器自由引导，注入缺失的数据集特定信息。

Result: 在11个基准测试上的广泛结果表明，DP-FM在少样本多步适应任务中取得了新的最优性能，有效消除了角度动态扭曲，同时保持了径向一致性并恢复了数据集特定信息。

Conclusion: WP-FM框架通过黎曼几何视角重新构建了跨模态对齐问题，提出的DP-FM方法解决了现有流匹配方法的关键限制，为视觉语言模型的少样本适应提供了更优的几何基础，显著提升了适应性能。

Abstract: Recent flow matching (FM) methods improve the few-shot adaptation of vision-language models, by modeling cross-modal alignment as a continuous multi-step flow. In this paper, we argue that existing FM methods are inherently constrained by incompatible geometric priors on pre-trained cross-modal features, resulting in suboptimal adaptation performance. We first analyze these methods from a polar decomposition perspective (i.e., radial and angular sub-manifolds). Under this new geometric view, we identify three overlooked limitations in them: 1) Angular dynamics distortion: The radial-angular coupling induces non-uniform speed on the angular sub-manifold, leading to regression training difficulty and extra truncation errors. 2) Radial dynamics neglect: Feature normalization discards modality confidence, failing to distinguish out-of-distribution and in-distribution data, and abandoning crucial radial dynamics. 3) Context-agnostic unconditional flow: Dataset-specific information loss during pre-trained cross-modal feature extraction remains unrecovered. To resolve these issues, we propose warped product flow matching (WP-FM), a unified Riemannian framework that reformulates alignment on a warped product manifold. Within this framework, we derive direct product flow matching (DP-FM) by introducing a constant-warping metric, which yields a decoupled cylindrical manifold (i.e., direct product manifold). DP-FM enables independent radial evolution and constant-speed angular geodesic transport, effectively eliminating angular dynamics distortion while preserving radial consistency. Meanwhile, we incorporate classifier-free guidance by conditioning the flow on the pre-trained VLMs' hidden states to inject missing dataset-specific information. Extensive results across 11 benchmarks have demonstrated that DP-FM achieves a new state-of-the-art for multi-step few-shot adaptation.

</details>


### [59] [VTAgent: Agentic Keyframe Anchoring for Evidence-Aware Video TextVQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04870)
*Haibin He,Maoyuan Ye,Jing Zhang,Juhua Liu,Bo Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种用于视频文本视觉问答的框架，通过问题引导的智能体明确锚定关键帧，显著提升了现有视频大模型在Video TextVQA任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管最近的视频大模型具备强大的多模态视频理解能力，但在现有Video TextVQA基准测试上的性能仍然有限。研究发现主要瓶颈在于定位关键问题相关证据的能力，而非推理能力本身。

Method: 提出问题引导的智能体框架：1）先进行关键帧上界分析，统计任意单帧给出正确答案的情况；2）明确锚定与问题相关的关键帧后再回答问题；3）可在无训练设置下有效工作，也可通过监督微调和强化学习进一步优化。

Result: 该方法在无训练设置下已优于直接视频推理，经过监督微调和强化学习后，在多个基准测试上平均提升了12.12%的准确率和11.15%的ANLS分数，创造了新的最优结果。

Conclusion: 研究表明明确的关键帧锚定对提升Video TextVQA性能至关重要。该框架有效解决了证据定位的瓶颈问题，为视频文本视觉问答提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Video text-based visual question answering (Video TextVQA) aims to answer questions by reasoning over visual textual content appearing in videos. Despite the strong multimodal video understanding capabilities of recent Video-LLMs, their performance on existing Video TextVQA benchmarks remains limited. To better understand this gap, we conduct an upper-bound analysis through frame-wise question answering, counting a sample as correct if any frame yields the right answer, which significantly outperforms direct video-based inference and reveals a substantial performance gap. The results suggest that the primary bottleneck lies in the localization of key question-relevant evidence, rather than in reasoning capacity itself. Building on this insight, we propose a question-guided agent framework that explicitly anchors the relevant keyframes before answering. The approach operates effectively in a training-free setting and consistently surpasses direct video inference. With additional supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), it achieves an average improvement of +12.12 in accuracy and +11.15 in ANLS across benchmarks, establishing new state-of-the-art results. Our study underscores the critical role of explicit keyframe anchoring for advancing Video TextVQA. The code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [60] [Aes3D: Aesthetic Assessment in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05155)
*Chuanzhi Xu,Boyu Wei,Haoxian Zhou,Xuanhua Yin,Zihan Deng,Haodong Chen,Qiang Qu,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Aes3D框架，首个系统评估3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)场景美学质量的框架，包含首个3D场景美学数据集Aesthetic3D和直接处理3DGS表示的美学评分模型Aes3DGSNet


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着3DGS在沉浸式媒体和数字内容创作中的应用增加，需要评估3D场景美学质量来帮助创作者构建更具视觉吸引力的内容。现有评估方法主要关注重建保真度和感知真实感，忽略了构图、和谐度、视觉吸引力等高层次美学属性

Method: 提出Aes3D框架：1)构建首个3D场景美学评估数据集Aesthetic3D，采用专门的3D场景美学标注策略；2)开发Aes3DGSNet轻量级模型，直接从3D高斯基元预测场景级美学评分，无需渲染多视角图像

Result: 通过多视角3DGS场景表示的美学监督学习，Aes3DGSNet能有效捕捉高层次美学线索并准确回归美学评分。实验结果表明该方法在保持轻量级设计的同时实现了强大性能

Conclusion: Aes3D为3D场景美学评估建立了新的基准，解决了现有方法忽视高层次美学属性的问题。该框架直接处理3DGS表示，减少了计算成本和硬件需求

Abstract: As 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) gains attention in immersive media and digital content creation, assessing the aesthetics of 3D scenes becomes important in helping creators build more visually compelling 3D content. However, existing evaluation methods for 3D scenes primarily emphasize reconstruction fidelity and perceptual realism, largely overlooking higher-level aesthetic attributes such as composition, harmony, and visual appeal. This limitation comes from two key challenges: (1) the absence of general 3DGS datasets with aesthetic annotations, and (2) the intrinsic nature of 3DGS as a low-level primitive representation, which makes it difficult to capture high-level aesthetic features. To address these challenges, we propose Aes3D, the first systematic framework for assessing the aesthetics of 3D neural rendering scenes. Aes3D includes Aesthetic3D, the first dataset dedicated to 3D scene aesthetic assessment, built on our proposed annotation strategy for 3D scene aesthetics. In addition, we present Aes3DGSNet, a lightweight model that directly predicts scene-level aesthetic scores from 3DGS representations. Notably, our model operates solely on 3D Gaussian primitives, eliminating the need for rendering multi-view images and thus reducing computational cost and hardware requirements. Through aesthetics-supervised learning on multi-view 3DGS scene representations, Aes3DGSNet effectively captures high-level aesthetic cues and accurately regresses aesthetic scores. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach achieves strong performance while maintaining a lightweight design, establishing a new benchmark for 3D scene aesthetic assessment. Code and datasets will be made available in a future version.

</details>


### [61] [ICPR 2026 Competition on Privacy-Preserving Person Re-Identification from Top-View RGB-Depth Camera (TVRID)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04977)
*Raphaël Delécluse,Hazem Wannous,Laurent Guimas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TVRID竞赛报告，介绍了顶视图、RGB-D深度数据、多模态行人重识别竞赛的设置、数据集和结果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立顶视图、深度感知和多模态行人重识别的可重复基准测试，解决隐私保护场景下的行人重识别问题

Method: 使用四个同步的Intel RealSense D455摄像头采集86个身份的RGB-Depth数据，包含平面、上升、下降和倾斜四种视角变化，通过服务器端统一评估三个赛道：RGB Re-ID、Depth Re-ID和RGB↔Depth跨模态检索

Result: 竞赛结果显示明确的难度排序：RGB > Depth > Cross-Modal，证明了模态约束检索的挑战性和模态不变学习的可行性

Conclusion: TVRID通过发布数据集、评估脚本和文档，为顶视图、深度感知和跨模态行人重识别建立了可重复的基准测试平台

Abstract: This companion paper reports the ICPR 2026 TVRID competition on privacy-aware top-view person re-identification. We present the competition setting, the released RGB-Depth dataset, and a summary of final results with descriptions of the top entries. TVRID contains 86 identities captured by four synchronized overhead Intel RealSense D455 cameras, with paired RGB/Depth streams and structured geometric variation across flat, ascent, descent, and oblique viewpoints. The evaluation protocol includes three tracks: RGB Re-ID, Depth Re-ID, and RGB$\leftrightarrow$Depth cross-modal retrieval. Submissions are ranked using mAP and CMC-1 under a unified server-side evaluation. The final results show a clear difficulty ordering (RGB $>$ Depth $>$ Cross-Modal), highlighting both the challenge of modality-constrained retrieval and the feasibility of strong performance with modality-invariant learning. By releasing the dataset at https://zenodo.org/records/17909410, the evaluation scripts at https://github.com/RaphaelDel/ICPR-TVRID, and the accompanying documentation, TVRID establishes a reproducible benchmark for top-view, depth-based, and cross-modal person re-id.

</details>


### [62] [Attention-Based Chaotic Self-Supervision for Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04985)
*Joao Batista Florindo,Amanda Pontes de Oliveira Ornelas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于医学图像分类的自监督学习预训练策略——混沌去噪自编码器（CDAE），通过应用混沌变换而非随机掩码来保留诊断特征，并结合注意力融合机制整合通用和领域特定特征，在皮肤病变和糖尿病视网膜病变数据集上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分类通常依赖大规模标注数据集或ImageNet迁移学习，而自监督学习虽是一种有力替代方案，但常用方法（如掩码自编码器）可能因随机掩码破坏细粒度诊断特征。需要一种能够保留医学图像关键诊断信息的自监督预训练方法。

Method: 提出混沌去噪自编码器（CDAE）：1）对输入图像应用混沌变换而非随机掩码，训练自编码器重构原始图像，迫使编码器学习领域特定特征；2）引入注意力融合机制，结合CDAE训练的编码器特征与标准编码器特征，充分利用通用和领域特定表示的优势。

Result: 在两个公共医学数据集上评估：ISIC 2018（皮肤病变）准确率0.9221，F1-macro 0.8530；APTOS 2019（糖尿病视网膜病变）准确率0.8644，F1-macro 0.7433。结果表明该方法在保留诊断特征方面优于传统掩码方法。

Conclusion: CDAE通过混沌变换有效避免了随机掩码对医学图像诊断特征的破坏，结合注意力融合机制能够整合不同特征表示的优势，为医学图像分类提供了一种有效的自监督预训练策略，在皮肤病变和糖尿病视网膜病变分类任务中表现出色。

Abstract: Deep learning models for medical image classification usually achieve promising results but typically rely on large, annotated datasets or standard transfer learning from ImageNet. Self-Supervised Learning (SSL) has emerged as a powerful alternative, yet common methods like masked autoencoders (MAEs) may inadvertently destroy fine-grained diagnostic features by using random masking. In this paper, we propose a novel SSL pre-training strategy, the Chaotic Denoising Autoencoder (CDAE). Instead of masking, we apply a chaotic transformation to the input image, tasking an autoencoder to reconstruct the original. We hypothesize this forces the encoder to learn robust, domain-specific features by "inverting the chaos". Furthermore, we propose an attentive fusion mechanism that combines features from our CDAE-trained encoder with a standard encoder, leveraging the strengths of both general and domain-specific representations. Our method is evaluated on two public medical datasets: ISIC 2018 (skin lesions) and APTOS 2019 (diabetic retinopathy). The proposed model achieves high performance, with an accuracy of 0.9221 and an F1-macro of 0.8530 on ISIC 2018, and an accuracy of 0.8644 and F1-macro of 0.7433 on APTOS 2019, demonstrating the efficacy of our approach.

</details>


### [63] [Low-Rank Adaptation of Geospatial Foundation Models for Wildfire Mapping Using Sentinel-2 Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04989)
*Ali Shibli,Andrea Nascetti,Yifang Ban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 评估三种地理空间基础模型（Terramind、DINOv3、Prithvi-v2）在美国和加拿大的野火烧毁区域制图中的应用，发现使用LoRA（低秩适应）参数高效调优方法能获得最佳跨域泛化性能，其中Prithvi-v2模型表现最优


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然近年来地理空间基础模型为卫星影像提供了强大的通用表示能力，但如何高效将这些模型适应到下游地球观测任务中，特别是在面对地理和时间域偏移时，仍缺乏明确的方法理解。野火烧毁区域制图对于损害评估、排放建模和理解不同生态区域的火-气候相互作用至关重要。

Method: 1. 评估三种最先进的地理空间基础模型：Terramind、DINOv3和Prithvi-v2；2. 使用2017-2023年3,820个野火事件的Sentinel-2数据；3. 在不同生物群落中进行空间和时间泛化测试；4. 系统比较三种适应方法：完整微调、仅解码器微调和低秩适应（LoRA）。

Result: 在所有实验中，LoRA提供了最强的跨域泛化能力，同时只更新不到1%的参数，在准确性和效率之间取得了有利的权衡。Prithvi-v2与LoRA结合实现了最高的整体准确性，并且相比完整微调获得了最大的改进。

Conclusion: 地理空间基础模型，当使用轻量级参数高效方法如LoRA进行适应时，为大尺度野火烧毁区域制图提供了稳健且可扩展的解决方案。这为地球观测任务中的模型适应策略提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Wildfire burned-area mapping is essential for damage assessment, emissions modeling, and understanding fire-climate interactions across diverse ecological regions. Recent geospatial foundation models provide strong general-purpose representations for satellite imagery, yet there is still no clear understanding of how to efficiently adapt these models for downstream Earth observation tasks, particularly under geographic and temporal domain shift. This study evaluates three state-of-the-art Geospatial Foundation Models (GFMs) - Terramind, DINOv3, and Prithvi-v2 - for burned-area mapping across the United States and Canada using Sentinel-2 data. Leveraging 3,820 wildfire events from 2017-2023, we conduct spatial and temporal generalization tests across diverse biomes. We systematically compare full fine-tuning, decoder-only fine-tuning, and Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) for adapting each model. Across all experiments, LoRA provides the strongest cross-domain generalization while updating less than 1% of parameters, demonstrating a favorable trade-off between accuracy and efficiency. Prithvi-v2 with LoRA achieves the highest overall accuracy and the largest improvement compared to full fine-tuning. These findings indicate that geospatial foundation models, when adapted using lightweight parameter-efficient methods such as LoRA, offer a robust and scalable solution for large-scale burned-area mapping. Code is available at https://github.com/alishibli97/wildfire-lora-gfm.

</details>


### [64] [Taming Outlier Tokens in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05206)
*Xiaoyu Wu,Yifei Wang,Tsu-Jui Fu,Liang-Chieh Chen,Zhe Gan,Chen Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究了扩散变换器(DiT)中的异常令牌问题，发现编码器和去噪器都会产生高范数异常令牌，这些令牌携带有限局部信息但吸引过多注意力。作者提出双阶段寄存器(DSR)干预方法来减少异常伪影并提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作发现视觉变换器(ViT)会产生少量高范数令牌，这些令牌吸引不成比例的关注但携带有限局部信息。然而，在生成模型中这些异常令牌的作用仍未充分探索，特别是在现代表示自编码器(RAE)-DiT流程中。

Method: 作者引入双阶段寄存器(DSR)干预方法：1) 训练时可用的情况下使用训练寄存器，否则使用递归测试时寄存器；2) 用于去噪器的扩散寄存器。这些寄存器基于干预策略来处理编码器和去噪器中的异常令牌问题。

Result: 在ImageNet和大规模文本到图像生成任务中，DSR干预方法能够一致地减少异常伪影并改善生成质量，证明了异常令牌控制是构建更强DiT的重要要素。

Conclusion: 研究表明异常令牌现象存在于现代RAE-DiT流程的两个组件中，简单地掩盖高范数令牌无法改善性能，因为问题更与损坏的局部块语义相关。通过寄存器干预方法可以有效解决这一问题，提升生成质量。

Abstract: We study outlier tokens in Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) for image generation. Prior work has shown that Vision Transformers (ViTs) can produce a small number of high-norm tokens that attract disproportionate attention while carrying limited local information, but their role in generative models remains underexplored. We show that this phenomenon appears in both the encoder and denoiser of modern Representation Autoencoder (RAE)-DiT pipelines: pretrained ViT encoders can produce outlier representations, and DiTs themselves can develop internal outlier tokens, especially in intermediate layers. Moreover, simply masking high-norm tokens does not improve performance, indicating that the problem is not only caused by a few extreme values, but is more closely related to corrupted local patch semantics. To address this issue, we introduce Dual-Stage Registers (DSR), a register-based intervention for both components: trained registers when available, recursive test-time registers otherwise, and diffusion registers for the denoiser. Across ImageNet and large-scale text-to-image generation, these interventions consistently reduce outlier artifacts and improve generation quality. Our results highlight outlier-token control as an important ingredient in building stronger DiTs.

</details>


### [65] [CARD: A Multi-Modal Automotive Dataset for Dense 3D Reconstruction in Challenging Road Topography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05014)
*Gasser Elazab,Frank Neuhaus,Tilman Koß,Malte Splietker,Aditya Date,Michael Unterreiner,Maximilian Jansen,Olaf Hellwich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CARD是一个多模态自动驾驶数据集，提供准稠密3D地面真实值，特别关注路面不规则区域，覆盖德国和意大利的110公里道路，包含丰富的速度颠簸、坑洼和越野路段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶数据集主要采集于平整良好道路，且提供的LiDAR地面真实值稀疏，不足以评估深度估计和补全中的精细几何结构，特别是在路面不规则区域。

Method: 使用同步全局快门立体相机、前后LiDAR、LiDAR惯性里程计6-DoF位姿、每轮运动轨迹和完整标定的传感器套件，通过多LiDAR融合技术生成每帧约500K有效深度像素的准稠密3D地面真实值。

Result: CARD数据集提供比KITTI深度补全数据集多6.5倍的有效深度像素，比其他公共驾驶数据集平均多10倍的深度信息，覆盖4.7小时、110公里的多样化道路场景，并提供针对路面地形不规则的2D边界框标注。

Conclusion: CARD填补了现有驾驶数据集在路面不规则区域和精细几何评估方面的空白，建立了标准化的路面不规则评估协议，为深度估计和感知任务提供了高质量的基准测试平台。

Abstract: Autonomous driving must operate across diverse surfaces to enable safe mobility. However, most driving datasets are captured on well-paved flat roads. Moreover, recent driving datasets primarily provide sparse LiDAR ground truth for images, which is insufficient for assessing fine-grained geometry in depth estimation and completion. To address these gaps, we introduce CARD, a multi-modal driving dataset that delivers quasi-dense 3D ground truth across continuous sequences rich in speed bumps, potholes, irregular surfaces and off-road segments. Our sensor suite includes synchronized global-shutter stereo cameras, front and rear LiDARs, 6-DoF poses from LiDAR-inertial odometry, per-wheel motion traces, and full calibration. Notably, our multi-LiDAR fusion yields ~500K valid depth pixels per frame, about 6.5x more than KITTI Depth Completion and 10x more on average than other public driving datasets. The dataset spans ~110 km and 4.7 hours across Germany and Italy. In addition, CARD provides 2D bounding boxes targeting road-topography irregularities, enabling accurate benchmarking for both geometry and perception tasks. Furthermore, we establish a standardized evaluation protocol for road surface irregularities on CARD and benchmark state-of-the-art depth estimation models to provide strong baselines. The CARD dataset is hosted on https://huggingface.co/CARD-Data.

</details>


### [66] [Prompt-Anchored Vision-Text Distillation for Lifelong Person Re-identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05027)
*Wen Wen,Hao Chen,Shiliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PAD是一种用于终身行人重识别的非对称视觉-文本蒸馏框架，利用预训练视觉-语言模型的文本编码器作为跨域语义锚点，通过提示蒸馏和自适应提示池实现语义对齐和域适应，平衡稳定性和可塑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终身行人重识别面临语义漂移、适应能力有限和灾难性遗忘问题。现有无示例方法主要依赖视觉蒸馏或参数正则化，忽略了文本等辅助模态在保持语义稳定性和实现增量可塑性方面的潜力。

Method: 提出Prompt-Anchored vision-text Distillation (PAD)：文本侧蒸馏提示以在固定语义空间中保持视觉-文本对齐；视觉侧使用基于EMA的教师模型和自适应提示池，通过分配新槽位同时冻结旧槽位实现域适应，解耦视觉和文本的角色。

Result: 大量实验表明，PAD在可见域和未见域上显著优于现有最先进方法，在稳定性和可塑性之间实现了良好平衡。

Conclusion: 预训练视觉-语言模型的冻结文本编码器可作为跨域语义锚点，PAD框架通过非对称视觉-文本蒸馏有效解决了终身行人重识别中的语义漂移和灾难性遗忘问题，展示了文本模态在保持语义稳定性和促进增量学习方面的重要价值。

Abstract: Lifelong person re-identification (LReID) aims to train a generalizable model with sequentially collected data. However, such models often suffer from semantic drift, limited adaptability, and catastrophic forgetting as new domains emerge. Existing exemplar-free approaches largely rely on visual-only distillation or parameter regularization, while overlooking the potential of auxiliary modalities, such as text, to preserve semantic stability and enable incremental plasticity. We observe that the frozen text encoder in pretrained vision-language models can serve as a stable semantic anchor across domains. To decouple the roles of vision and text, we propose Prompt-Anchored vision-text Distillation (PAD), an asymmetric vision-text framework for semantic alignment and cross-domain generalization. On the textual side, we distill prompts to preserve vision-text alignment under a fixed semantic space, acting as a global semantic reference rather than a dominant learning signal. On the visual side, an EMA-based teacher with an adaptive prompt pool enables domain-wise adaptation by allocating new slots while freezing past ones. Extensive experiments show that PAD substantially outperforms state-of-the-art methods across seen and unseen domains, achieving a strong balance between stability and plasticity. Project page is available at https://github.com/zu-zi/PAD.

</details>


### [67] [ScriptHOI: Learning Scripted State Transitions for Open-Vocabulary Human-Object Interaction Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05057)
*Minh Anh Nguyen,Quang Huy Tran,Bao Ngoc Le,SuiYang Guang,Tuan Kiet Pham,Linh Chi Vo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScriptHOI：一个结构化框架，将HOI检测重新构架为软脚本状态转换过程，通过分解交互短语到多个语义槽并进行视觉状态解析，减少对象可负担性和短语共现的误导预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的开放词汇人类-物体交互检测方法主要依靠人类-物体特征与文本嵌入的匹配，但其预测往往被对象可负担性和短语级共现主导，导致模型可能仅根据物体存在（如刀和蛋糕）预测交互（如"切蛋糕"），而未能验证手、工具、目标、接触模式和物体状态是否共同支持该动作。

Method: 1. 将每个交互短语表示为软脚本状态转换，分解为身体角色、接触、几何、可负担性、运动和物体状态六个语义槽
2. 使用视觉状态分词器解析检测到的人类-物体对到对应的状态标记
3. 槽级匹配器评估脚本覆盖度和脚本冲突度，用于校准HOI逻辑分数、暴露缺失的视觉证据并提供训练约束
4. 引入区间部分标签学习，为未标注候选项设定脚本推导的概率上下界而非分配封闭世界负样本
5. 使用反事实脚本对比损失交换单个脚本槽以抑制仅依赖对象的捷径

Result: 在HICO-DET、V-COCO和开放词汇HOI分割上的实验表明，ScriptHOI在罕见和未见交互识别方面有显著改进，同时大幅减少了可负担性冲突导致的误报。

Conclusion: 通过将HOI检测构架为结构化状态转换过程而非简单的特征匹配，ScriptHOI能够更精确地验证交互的视觉证据，提高了开放词汇HOI检测的准确性和鲁棒性，特别是在处理罕见类别和避免对象可负担性误导方面表现出色。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary human-object interaction (HOI) detection requires recognizing interaction phrases that may not appear as annotated categories during training. Recent vision-language HOI detectors improve semantic transfer by matching human-object features with text embeddings, but their predictions are often dominated by object affordance and phrase-level co-occurrence. As a result, a model may predict \textit{cut cake} from the presence of a knife and a cake without verifying whether the hand, tool, target, contact pattern, and object state jointly support the action. We propose \textbf{ScriptHOI}, a structured framework that represents each interaction phrase as a soft scripted state transition. Rather than treating a phrase as a single class token, ScriptHOI decomposes it into body-role, contact, geometry, affordance, motion, and object-state slots. A visual state tokenizer parses each detected human-object pair into corresponding state tokens, and a slot-wise matcher estimates both script coverage and script conflict. These two quantities calibrate HOI logits, expose missing visual evidence, and provide training constraints for incomplete annotations. To avoid suppressing valid but unannotated interactions, we further introduce interval partial-label learning, which constrains unannotated candidates with script-derived lower and upper probability bounds instead of assigning closed-world negatives. A counterfactual script contrast loss swaps individual script slots to discourage object-only shortcuts. Experiments on HICO-DET, V-COCO, and open-vocabulary HOI splits show that ScriptHOI improves rare and unseen interaction recognition while substantially reducing affordance-conflict false positives.

</details>


### [68] [Height-Guided Projection Reparameterization for Camera-LiDAR Occupancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05072)
*Yuan Wu,Zhiqiang Yan,Jiawei Lian,Zhengxue Wang,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiPR：一种相机-LiDAR联合的3D占据预测框架，通过高度引导的投影重参数化解决传统固定投影空间在捕捉真实场景稀疏性和高度变化方面的不足，实现了自适应的特征聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D占据预测方法主要依赖固定投影空间，即在柱状体上均匀采样3D参考点。然而这种采样方式难以捕捉真实场景的稀疏性和高度变化，导致特征对应关系模糊和特征聚合不可靠。

Method: HiPR首先将LiDAR点云编码为BEV高度图以捕捉点云的最大高度；然后利用高度先验调整每个柱状体的采样范围，实现投影空间的自适应重参数化；同时掩蔽高度图中的无效部分；引入训练时渐进式高度调节策略，逐步从真实高度过渡到LiDAR高度，缓解训练不稳定性。

Result: 大量实验表明，HiPR在保持实时推理的同时，持续超越现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: HiPR通过高度引导的投影重参数化机制，有效解决了传统固定投影空间在特征聚合中的局限性，为3D占据预测提供了更精确、更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: 3D occupancy prediction aims to infer dense, voxel-wise scene semantics from sensor observations, where the 2D-to-3D view transformation serves as a crucial step in bridging image features and volumetric representations. Most previous methods rely on a fixed projection space, where 3D reference points are uniformly sampled along pillars. However, such sampling struggles to capture the sparsity and height variations of real-world scenes, leading to ambiguous correspondences and unreliable feature aggregation. To address these challenges, we propose HiPR, a camera-LiDAR occupancy framework with Height-Guided Projection Reparameterization. HiPR first encodes LiDAR into a BEV height map to capture the maximum height of the point cloud. HiPR then adjusts the sampling range of each pillar using the height prior, enabling adaptive reparameterization of the projection space. As a result, the projected points are redistributed into geometrically meaningful regions rather than fixed ranges. Meanwhile, we mask out the invalid parts of the height map to avoid misleading the feature aggregation. In addition, to alleviate the training instability caused by noisy LiDAR-derived heights, we introduce a training-time Progressive Height Conditioning strategy, which gradually transitions the conditioning signal from ground-truth heights to LiDAR heights. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HiPR consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods while maintaining real-time inference. The code and pretrained models can be found at https://github.com/Rayn-Wu/HiPR.

</details>


### [69] [FlowDIS: Language-Guided Dichotomous Image Segmentation with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05077)
*Andranik Sargsyan,Shant Navasardyan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowDIS是一种基于流匹配框架的二值图像分割方法，通过时间相关的向量场将图像分布传输到掩码分布，并可通过文本提示进行控制，在DIS任务上达到最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二值图像分割（DIS）方法在保留细粒度细节和完全捕获前景语义结构方面存在不足，需要一种既能提供精确像素级分割又具有强可控性的方法。

Method: 提出FlowDIS方法，基于流匹配框架学习时间相关的向量场，将图像分布传输到对应的掩码分布，并可选择性地以文本提示为条件。同时设计了位置感知实例配对（PAIP）训练策略，增强文本提示的控制能力。

Result: 在DIS-TE测试集上，FlowDIS比之前最好的DIS方法在F_β^ω指标上提高了5.5%，MAE（M）降低了43%，无论有无语言指导都显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: FlowDIS通过流匹配框架和PAIP训练策略，实现了高质量的二值图像分割，提供了通过文本提示进行精确像素级对象分割的强大可控性，在DIS任务上达到最先进的性能。

Abstract: Accurate image segmentation is essential for modern computer vision applications such as image editing, autonomous driving, and medical image analysis. In recent years, Dichotomous Image Segmentation (DIS) has become a standard task for training and evaluating highly accurate segmentation models. Existing DIS approaches often fail to preserve fine-grained details or fully capture the semantic structure of the foreground. To address these challenges, we present FlowDIS, a novel dichotomous image segmentation method built on the flow matching framework, which learns a time-dependent vector field to transport the image distribution to the corresponding mask distribution, optionally conditioned on a text prompt. Moreover, with our Position-Aware Instance Pairing (PAIP) training strategy, FlowDIS offers strong controllability through text prompts, enabling precise, pixel-level object segmentation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches both with and without language guidance. Compared with the best prior DIS method, FlowDIS achieves a 5.5% higher $F_β^ω$ measure and 43% lower MAE ($\mathcal{M}$) on the DIS-TE test set. The code is available at: https://github.com/Picsart-AI-Research/FlowDIS

</details>


### [70] [A unified Benchmark for Multi-Frame Image Restoration under Severe Refractive Warping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05079)
*Maxim V. Shugaev,Md Reshad Ul Hoque,Bridget Kennedy,Joseph T. Riley,Fiona Hwang,Justin Hagen,Harvir Ghuman,Ethan Garcia-O'Donnell,Syed Noor Qadri,Freddie Santiago,Mun Wai Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个用于视频几何畸变去除的全面基准测试，涵盖了从温和湍流到强烈非均匀折射变形的各种失真条件，包含实验室真实数据和合成序列，并评估了从传统方法到先进学习方法的多种算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频序列通过折射动态介质（如湍流空气或水面）捕获时存在严重几何畸变和时间不稳定性。虽然最近的研究解决了轻微大气湍流问题，但缺乏系统评估在强非均匀折射条件下的恢复方法的基准测试。需要建立能全面评估各种失真水平的基准测试框架。

Method: 1. 建立了全面的几何畸变去除基准测试，覆盖从湍流样轻度变形到强间断折射变形的范围；2. 包括实验室捕获的真实数据和基于物理光折射模型生成的合成序列；3. 涵盖四种失真水平和多种表面波类型；4. 评估了从简单基线、经典配准算法到先进学习方法（如DATUM和提出的扩散基V-cache）的多种方法；5. 使用像素级（PSNR、SSIM）和感知（LPIPS、DINO、CLIP）指标进行评估。

Result: 该基准测试首次对几何畸变去除进行了大规模分析，建立了从高度失真光学环境中重建视频的算法开发和评估的新基础。论文提供了代码和数据集，为相关研究提供了标准化评估平台。

Conclusion: 提出的基准测试为在强折射条件下开发视频几何畸变去除算法提供了系统评估框架，填补了现有研究在强非均匀失真条件下的评估空白，建立了从高度失真光学环境重建视频的新标准。

Abstract: Video sequence capturing through refractive dynamic media, such as a turbulent air or water surface, often suffer from severe geometric distortions and temporal instability. While recent advances address mild atmospheric turbulence, no existing benchmarks systematically evaluate restoration methods under strong and highly nonuniform refractive conditions. We present a comprehensive benchmark for geometric distortion removal in video, covering a range from turbulence-like mild warping to strong discontinuous refractive deformations. The benchmark includes both laboratory-captured real data and synthetic sequences generated for static scenes via physics-based light refraction modeling across four distortion levels and multiple surface wave types. We evaluate a spectrum of methods from simple baselines and classical registration algorithms to advanced learning-based approaches including DATUM and our proposed diffusion based V-cache for high and extreme distortions regimes. Evaluation uses both pixel-level (PSNR, SSIM), and perceptual (LPIPS, DINO, CLIP) metrics providing the first large scale analysis of geometric distortion removal. Our benchmark establishes a new foundation for developing and evaluating algorithms capable of reconstructing video from highly distorted optical environments. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/iafoss/refractive-mfir-benchmark.

</details>


### [71] [Wasserstein-Aligned Localisation for VLM-Based Distributional OOD Detection in Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05161)
*Bernhard Kainz,Johanna P Mueller,Matthew Baugh,Cosmin Bercea*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WALDO是一个基于最优传输理论的免训练框架，通过结构化比较正常解剖参考分布来实现零样本异常定位，在NOVA脑MRI基准测试中实现了19%的相对性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视觉语言模型的零样本异常定位方法在罕见病理检测方面具有吸引力，但其性能受到缺乏健康解剖学上下文的根本限制。现有的零样本方法未能充分利用正常解剖分布作为比较参考。

Method: 将零样本定位重新表述为比较推理问题，通过最优传输理论构建WALDO框架：1)使用熵加权切片Wasserstein距离从DINOv2补丁分布中选取解剖感知的参考；2)利用参考相似性与定位精度之间的非单调关系进行Goldilocks区域采样；3)通过加权非极大值抑制实现自一致性聚合。

Result: 在NOVA脑MRI基准测试中，WALDO配合Qwen2.5-VL-72B达到43.5±1.6%的mAP@30（95%CI：[40.4, 46.7]），相比零样本基线有19%的相对提升。跨模型评估显示一致增益，配对McNemar测试确认统计显著性(p<0.01)。

Conclusion: 通过将零样本异常定位重新构建为比较推理问题，并利用最优传输理论选择适度相似的解剖参考分布，WALDO框架有效克服了缺乏健康解剖上下文的限制，显著提升了罕见病理检测的定位精度。

Abstract: Zero-shot anomaly localisation via vision-language models (VLMs) offers a compelling approach for rare pathology detection, yet its performance is fundamentally limited by the absence of healthy anatomical context. We reformulate zero-shot localisation as a comparative inference problem in which anomalies are identified through structured comparison against reference distributions of normal anatomy. We introduce WALDO, a training-free framework grounded in optimal transport theory that enables comparative reasoning through: (i) entropy-weighted Sliced Wasserstein distances for anatomically-aware reference selection from DINOv2 patch distributions, (ii) Goldilocks zone sampling exploiting the non-monotonic relationship between reference similarity and localisation accuracy, and (iii) self-consistency aggregation via weighted non-maximum suppression. We theoretically analyse the Goldilocks effect through distributional divergence, and show that references with moderate similarity minimize a bias-variance trade-off in comparative visual reasoning. On the NOVA brain MRI benchmark, WALDO with Qwen2.5-VL-72B achieves $43.5_{\pm1.6}\%$ mAP@30 (95\% CI: [40.4, 46.7]), representing a 19\% relative improvement over zero-shot baselines. Cross-model evaluation shows consistent gains: GPT-4o achieves $32.0_{\pm6.5}\%$ and Qwen3-VL-32B achieves $32.0_{\pm6.6}\%$ mAP@30. Paired McNemar tests confirm statistical significance ($p<0.01$). Source code is available at https://github.com/bkainz/WALDO_MICCAI26_demo .

</details>


### [72] [PhysForge: Generating Physics-Grounded 3D Assets for Interactive Virtual World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05163)
*Yunhan Yang,Chunshi Wang,Junliang Ye,Yang Li,Zanxin Chen,Zehuan Huang,Yao Mu,Zhuo Chen,Chunchao Guo,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysForge是一个两阶段框架，用于合成基于物理的3D交互资产，通过物理蓝图规划和物理基础扩散模型生成仿真就绪的资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前3D资产生成方法主要关注静态几何，忽略了交互所需的功能属性。交互资产生成需要基于功能逻辑和层次化物理，但现有方法存在这一空白。

Method: PhysForge采用解耦的两阶段框架：1）VLM作为"物理架构师"规划"层次化物理蓝图"，定义材料、功能和运动约束；2）基于物理的扩散模型通过新颖的KineVoxel注入机制实现蓝图，合成高保真几何和精确运动参数。框架基于PhysDB数据集（15万个具有四层物理标注的资产）。

Result: 实验表明，PhysForge能够生成功能上合理、仿真就绪的资产，为交互式3D内容和具身智能体提供了强大的数据引擎。

Conclusion: PhysForge框架成功地将功能逻辑和层次化物理融入3D资产生成过程，解决了现有方法忽略交互功能属性的问题，为构建互动虚拟世界和具身AI提供了重要技术支持。

Abstract: Synthesizing physics-grounded 3D assets is a critical bottleneck for interactive virtual worlds and embodied AI. Existing methods predominantly focus on static geometry, overlooking the functional properties essential for interaction. We propose that interactive asset generation must be rooted in functional logic and hierarchical physics. To bridge this gap, we introduce PhysForge, a decoupled two-stage framework supported by PhysDB, a large-scale dataset of 150,000 assets with four-tier physical annotations. First, a VLM acts as a "physical architect" to plan a "Hierarchical Physical Blueprint" defining material, functional, and kinematic constraints. Second, a physics-grounded diffusion model realizes this blueprint by synthesizing high-fidelity geometry alongside precise kinematic parameters via a novel KineVoxel Injection (KVI) mechanism. Experiments demonstrate that PhysForge produces functionally plausible, simulation-ready assets, providing a robust data engine for interactive 3D content and embodied agents.

</details>


### [73] [LoViF 2026 The First Challenge on Holistic Quality Assessment for 4D World Model (PhyScore)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05187)
*Wei Luo,Yiting Lu,Xin Li,Haoran Li,Fengbin Guan,Chen Gao,Xin Jin,Yong Li,Zhibo Chen,Sijing Wu,Kang Fu,Yunhao Li,Ziang Xiao,Huiyu Duan,Jing Liu,Qiang Hu,Xiongkuo Min,Guangtao Zhai,Manxi Sun,Zixuan Guo,Yun Li,Ziyang Chen,Manabu Tsukada,Zhengyang Li,Zhenglin Du,Yi Wen,Licheng Jiao,Fang Liu,Lingling Li,Yiwen Ren,Zhilong Song,Dubing Chen,Yucheng Zhou,Tianyi Yan,Huan Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoViF 2026 PhyScore挑战赛旨在评估世界模型生成视频的全面质量，超越传统感知质量评估，重点关注物理合理性、时间一致性和输入条件一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估实践中存在重要空白：仅凭感知质量不足以判断生成动态是否物理合理、时间一致且与输入条件一致。需要更全面的评估方法来衡量世界模型生成视频的真实物理特性和时序连贯性。

Method: 挑战赛要求参与者构建联合预测四个维度的指标：视频质量、物理真实性、条件-视频对齐性和时间一致性。此外还需定位物理异常时间戳以进行细粒度诊断。评估基于包含1,554个视频的基准数据集，这些视频由7个代表性世界生成模型生成，分为3个赛道（文本到2D、图像到4D、视频到4D）和26个类别。

Result: 建立了涵盖物理学相关场景（包括动力学、光学和热力学）以及多样现实世界和创意内容的全面基准数据集。评估协议结合时间戳IOU和SRCC/PLCC，既考虑分数预测也考虑异常定位。报告总结了挑战设计和提交解决方案的方法级洞察。

Conclusion: 该挑战赛为世界模型生成视频的全面质量评估建立了新的基准，推动了超越传统感知质量评估的多维度评估框架发展，特别强调物理合理性和时间一致性评估。
